=== idx: 468 ===
Gold supporting: during xci, one x is randomly silenced by xist.
LLM supporting:  the transcript responsible for x - chromosome inactivation is xist. xist, or x - inactive specific transcript, is a long non - coding rna that plays a crucial role in the regulation of x chromosome inactivation ( xci ). it is transcribed from the x chromosome and its expression is silenced on one of the two x chromosomes in female mammals, leading to the inactivation of that x chromosome. this process ensures dosage compensation between males and females by inactivating one x chromosome in females, thereby balancing the expression of x - linked genes. the silencing of xist is influenced by various factors, including the allele - specific expression of tsix, which acts in an antisense manner to repress xist. recent studies have also identified the xite element, which harbors intergenic transcription start sites and dnasei hypersensitive sites, contributing to the regulation of tsix and, consequently, xist expression. the interaction between these elements, particularly the role of xite in promoting tsix persistence on the active x chromosome, further influences the xci process, leading to x chromosome inequality and affecting the penetrance of x - linked diseases in females.
